| B080<br>subgrid 논문과의 일관성 | [docs/developments.rst:162](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:162) — <code>&#42; Improvements subgrid mode through added checks on input file, subgrid mode consistent with SFINCS subgrid paper: van Ormondt et al. (2025): https://doi.org/10.5194/gmd-18-843-2025.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:102](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:102) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;inq&#95;dimid(net&#95;file&#95;sbg%ncid, 'levels', net&#95;file&#95;sbg%nlevels&#95;dimid))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:108](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:108) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;inquire&#95;dimension(net&#95;file&#95;sbg%ncid, net&#95;file&#95;sbg%nlevels&#95;dimid, len = subgrid&#95;nlevels)) ! nr of levels</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:352](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:352) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;get&#95;var(net&#95;file&#95;sbg%ncid, net&#95;file&#95;sbg%uv&#95;pwet&#95;varid, rtmpuv2(:,:) ))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:356](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:356) — <code>subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(ilevel, ip) = rtmpuv2(ilevel, uv&#95;index(ip))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378) — <code>phi    = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip) + (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv + 1, ip) - subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip)) &#42; facint   ! wet fraction</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 코드가 수량·대표 수심·조도·젖은 비율의 표를 사용한다. 논문 전체와의 일관성은 범위 밖 논문 원문이 있어야 판정할 수 있다. |
| B081<br>stopdepth의 제거 | [docs/developments.rst:173](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:173) — <code>&#42; stopdepth - REMOVED in SFINCS v2.2.0, replaced by 'uvmax' to determine possible instabilities based on flow velocities rather than maximum water depth!</code> | [source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1530](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1530) — <code>! dtmin = alfa &#42; dxymin / (sqrt(9.81 &#42; stopdepth))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1539](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1539) — <code>dtmin = alfa &#42; dxymin / (1.25 &#42; abs(uvmax))</code> | **문서에만** | 확인: 현재 입력 리더에 stopdepth가 없다. 확인: uvmax 기반 식이 현재 사용된다. 문서 내부의 제거 판 번호가 다르다. 이전 v2.x tree가 있어야 제거 시점을 판정할 수 있다. |
| B082<br>Coriolis가 켜지는 투영좌표 위도 조건 | [docs/developments.rst:179](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:179) — <code>&#42; coriolis - clarification of use in model and logfile: for projected coordinate systems only turned on if a latitude is provided other than 0 (default, latitude = 0.0, means no coriolis terms used in momentum equation). For large scale applications on spherical grid, the coriolis term is turned on by default.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:105](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:105) — <code>call read&#95;logical&#95;input(500, 'coriolis', coriolis, .true.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:406](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:406) — <code>if (latitude &lt; 0.01 .and. latitude &gt; -0.01) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:410](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:410) — <code>coriolis = .false.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1471](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1471) — <code>fcorio2d(nm) = 2 &#42; 7.2921e-05 &#42; sin(z&#95;yz(nm) &#42; pi / 180)</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 구면좌표의 공간별 Coriolis 계산은 대응한다. 확인: 투영좌표에서는 -0.01 < latitude < 0.01이면 끈다. 문서의 'other than 0' 조건과 코드의 조건이 다르다. |
| B083<br>수위·수량의 double precision | [docs/developments.rst:180](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:180) — <code>&#42; waterlevel 'zs' and volume 'z&#95;volume' internal variables in SFINCS kernel are now stored as double precision.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_data.f90:586](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_data.f90:586) — <code>real&#42;8, dimension(:),   allocatable, target :: zs</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_data.f90:593](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_data.f90:593) — <code>real&#42;8, dimension(:),   allocatable :: z&#95;volume</code> | **일치** | 확인: zs와 z_volume이 real*8이다. 다른 출력·중간 배열까지 모두 real*8이라는 뜻은 아니다. |
| B084<br>배수 구조물의 건조 셀 NaN 방지 | [docs/developments.rst:184](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:184) — <code>&#42; Bugfix structures (pump, culvert, valve) so they cannot fall dry to NaNs in case the sink term cell becomes fully dry.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:396](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:396) — <code>if (zs(nmin)&gt;zs(nmout)) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:398](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:398) — <code>qq  = drainage&#95;params(idrn, 1) &#42; sqrt(zs(nmin) - zs(nmout))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:402) — <code>qq  = -drainage&#95;params(idrn, 1) &#42; sqrt(zs(nmout) - zs(nmin))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:632](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:632) — <code>qq = min(qq, max(z&#95;volume(nmin), 0.0) / dt)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:634](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:634) — <code>qq = max(qq, -max(z&#95;volume(nmout), 0.0) / dt)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:640](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:640) — <code>qq = min(qq, max((zs(nmin) - zb(nmin)) &#42; cell&#95;area(z&#95;flags&#95;iref(nmin)), 0.0) / dt)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:642](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:642) — <code>qq = max(qq, -max((zs(nmout) - zb(nmout)) &#42; cell&#95;area(z&#95;flags&#95;iref(nmout)), 0.0) / dt)</code> | **일치** | 확인: 펌프·암거·역류방지 밸브의 유량이 이용 가능한 양수 물량으로 제한된다. 수정 전후 결과와 모든 입력에서 NaN이 없다는 보장은 실행 검증이 필요하다. |
| B085<br>이류 수정의 실사례 영향이 작음 | [docs/developments.rst:185](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:185) — <code>&#42; Bugfix advection scheme. Influence on real world application cases in our testbed is small/negligible.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:448) — <code>elseif (advection&#95;scheme == 1) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:477](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:477) — <code>if (qd &gt; 1.0e-6) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:505](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:505) — <code>adv = min(max(adv, - advlim &#42; hu), advlim &#42; hu)</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 현재 코드에 이류 계산과 제한 식이 있다. 실사례 영향의 크기는 이전 소스와 testbed 결과가 있어야 판정할 수 있다. |
| B086<br>비정수압 압력 보정 | [docs/developments.rst:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:192) — <code>&#42; Nonhydrostatic pressure correction (for tsunami wave modelling), keywords nonh = yes (default no) and 'nh&#95;tstop', 'nh&#95;fnudge', 'nh&#95;tol', 'nh&#95;itermax'. Also added option to specify 'nonh&#95;mask' to turn on nonh correction only in part of the domain.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:191](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:191) — <code>call read&#95;logical&#95;input(500, 'nonh', nonhydrostatic, .false.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:192) — <code>call read&#95;real&#95;input(500, 'nh&#95;fnudge', nh&#95;fnudge, 0.9)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:193) — <code>call read&#95;real&#95;input(500, 'nh&#95;tstop', nh&#95;tstop, -999.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:194](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:194) — <code>call read&#95;real&#95;input(500, 'nh&#95;tol', nh&#95;tol, 0.001)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:195](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:195) — <code>call read&#95;int&#95;input(500, 'nh&#95;itermax', nh&#95;itermax, 100)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:411](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:411) — <code>status = NF90&#95;INQ&#95;VARID(net&#95;file&#95;qtr%ncid, "nonh&#95;mask", net&#95;file&#95;qtr%nonh&#95;mask&#95;varid)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:423](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:423) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;get&#95;var(net&#95;file&#95;qtr%ncid, net&#95;file&#95;qtr%nonh&#95;mask&#95;varid,  quadtree&#95;nonh&#95;mask(:)))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:1](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:1) — <code>! Non-hydrostatic code now only works with regular grids (can still use quadtree netcdf file as long as there are no refinement levels).</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:631](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:631) — <code>call bicgstab&#95;solve(nrows, AA, col&#95;idx, row&#95;ptr, QQ, pnh, nh&#95;tol, nh&#95;itermax, iter, relres, .true.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:664](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:664) — <code>q(ipuv)  = (1.0 - nh&#95;fnudge) &#42; q(ipuv) + nh&#95;fnudge &#42; (q(ipuv) + hu &#42; unh)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:665](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_nonhydrostatic.f90:665) — <code>uv(ipuv) = (1.0 - nh&#95;fnudge) &#42; uv(ipuv) + nh&#95;fnudge &#42; (uv(ipuv) + unh)</code> | **일치** | 확인: 압력 행렬 풀이와 유량·유속 보정이 대응한다. 확인: nonh_mask는 NetCDF 변수이다. 확인: 현재 소스 주석이 정칙 격자라는 제약을 적는다. BiCGStab-ILU 의존성 내부와 분산 오차는 이 대조에서 확인하지 않았다. |
| B087<br>h^(7/3) 표 | [docs/developments.rst:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:193) — <code>&#42; Added lookup table for h^(7/3) term in momentum equation. Potentially faster. Keyword h73table = 1/yes/true (default: false).</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:196](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:196) — <code>call read&#95;logical&#95;input(500, 'h73table', h73table, .false.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:663](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:663) — <code>if (h73table) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:667](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:667) — <code>hu73 = power7over3(max(hu, 1.0e-6))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:673](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:673) — <code>hu73 = hu&#42;&#42;2 &#42; hu&#42;&#42;expo</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:789](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:789) — <code>! Computes hu^(7/3) using a table for hu &lt; 10^4 and hu^(7/3) for hu &gt;= 10^4</code> | **일치** | 확인: 표 선택과 직접 계산 선택이 대응한다. 잠재적인 속도 향상은 실행 시간이 있어야 판정할 수 있다. |
| B088<br>subgrid의 평균 수심 출력 | [docs/developments.rst:194](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:194) — <code>&#42; Added option to store subgrid hmean (rather than zs - z&#95;zmin), keyword storehmean = yes (default no)</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:284](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:284) — <code>call read&#95;logical&#95;input(500, 'storehmean', store&#95;hmean, .false.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3541](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3541) — <code>if (store&#95;hmean .and. subgrid .eqv. .true.) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3547](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3547) — <code>call compute&#95;subgrid&#95;mean&#95;depth(zsmax, hmean)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3563](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3563) — <code>zstmp(m, n) = hmean(nm)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:4365](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:4365) — <code>hmean(nm) = volume / cell&#95;area&#95;m2(nm)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:4369](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:4369) — <code>hmean(nm) = volume / cell&#95;area(z&#95;flags&#95;iref(nm))</code> | **일치** | 확인: 코드는 표에서 최대 수위에 대응하는 수량을 구한다. 확인: 셀 면적으로 나눠 평균 수심을 출력한다. 확인: storehsubgrid=1 조건도 필요하다. |
| B089<br>upw1을 이류 기본값으로 사용 | [docs/developments.rst:217](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:217) — <code>&#42; New advection scheme 'advection&#95;scheme = upw1' &gt; new default! Old implementation still available as 'advection&#95;scheme = original'.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:178](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:178) — <code>call read&#95;char&#95;input(500,'advection&#95;scheme',advstr,'upw1')</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:672](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:672) — <code>advection&#95;scheme = 1</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:676](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:676) — <code>if (trim(advstr) == 'original') then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:677](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:677) — <code>advection&#95;scheme = 0</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:679](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:679) — <code>elseif (trim(advstr) == 'upw1') then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:422](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:422) — <code>if (advection&#95;scheme == 0) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:448) — <code>elseif (advection&#95;scheme == 1) then</code> | **일치** | 확인: upw1이 기본값이다. 확인: original 선택도 현재 남아 있다. |
| B090<br>2차원 마찰 성분 선택 | [docs/developments.rst:218](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:218) — <code>&#42; Option to include 2D component of friction term 'friction2d = true' &gt; new default! Old implementation still available as 'friction2d = false'.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:186](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:186) — <code>call read&#95;logical&#95;input(500,'friction2d',friction2d,.true.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:620](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:620) — <code>if (friction2d) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:624](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:624) — <code>qfr = sqrt(qx&#95;nm&#42;&#42;2 + (hu &#42; vu)&#42;&#42;2)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:630](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:630) — <code>qfr = abs(qx&#95;nm) ! flux to be used in the friction term</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:677](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:677) — <code>q(ip) = (qsm + frc &#42; dt) / (1.0 + gnavg2 &#42; dt &#42; qfr / hu73)</code> | **일치** | 확인: friction2d 기본값 true가 대응한다. 확인: 켜면 직교 유량 성분을 포함한 크기를 마찰 식에 사용한다. |
| B091<br>새 subgrid 젖은 비율과 구 binary의 1 | [docs/developments.rst:220](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:220) — <code>&#42; New subgrid methodology including wet fraction as in Van Ormondt et al. (2024, in review), only available through net netcdf input file through new HydroMT-SFINCS version, recommended! Old implementation still available when providing the original binary file, then all wet fractions are assumed to be 1.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:352](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:352) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;get&#95;var(net&#95;file&#95;sbg%ncid, net&#95;file&#95;sbg%uv&#95;pwet&#95;varid, rtmpuv2(:,:) ))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:356](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:356) — <code>subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(ilevel, ip) = rtmpuv2(ilevel, uv&#95;index(ip))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:515](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:515) — <code>subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet  = 1.0</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:698](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:698) — <code>subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet  = 1.0</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378) — <code>phi    = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip) + (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv + 1, ip) - subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip)) &#42; facint   ! wet fraction</code> | **일치** | 확인: NetCDF 입력에서 uv_pwet를 읽는다. 확인: 구 binary 분기에서는 표 전체의 젖은 비율을 1.0으로 초기화한다. |

(2024).</code> | [source/src/sfincs_snapwave.f90:543](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_snapwave.f90:543) — <code>call update&#95;boundary&#95;conditions(t) ! | B093<br>alpha·theta·advection·viscosity 권고 조합 | [docs/developments.rst:222](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:222) — <code>&#42; New recommended default combination that with new advection scheme: alpha=0.50, theta=1.0, advection=1 (is now always 2D), viscosity=1.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:70](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:70) — <code>call read&#95;real&#95;input(500,'alpha',alfa,0.50)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:71](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:71) — <code>call read&#95;real&#95;input(500,'theta',theta,1.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:90](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:90) — <code>call read&#95;int&#95;input(500,'advection',iadvection,1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:111) — <code>call read&#95;logical&#95;input(500,'viscosity',iviscosity,.false.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:590](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:590) — <code>viscosity = .false.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:591](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:591) — <code>if (iviscosity) then</code> | **일치** | 확인: alpha=0.5·theta=1·advection=1·viscosity=1을 입력할 수 있다. 문서는 권고 조합을 적는다. 확인: viscosity의 실제 읽기 기본값은 false이다. 권고와 자동 기본값을 구분해야 한다. |
| B094<br>dry→wet 셀 안정성 개선 | [docs/developments.rst:226](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:226) — <code>&#42; Improved stability for cell that from being dry becomes wet.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:616](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:616) — <code>qfr = sqrt(abs(dzdx) / (max(gnavg2, 1.0e-5) / 10)) &#42; hu &#42;&#42; (5.0 / 3.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:624](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:624) — <code>qfr = sqrt(qx&#95;nm&#42;&#42;2 + (hu &#42; vu)&#42;&#42;2)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:677](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:677) — <code>q(ip) = (qsm + frc &#42; dt) / (1.0 + gnavg2 &#42; dt &#42; qfr / hu73)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:612](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:612) — <code>if (kfuv(ip) == 0) then</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 새로 젖은 면에서 평형 유량 추정값을 사용한다. 개선 전후 안정성은 이전 소스와 시험 결과가 있어야 판정할 수 있다. |
| B095<br>단일 레벨 mesh 출력 선택 | [docs/developments.rst:228](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:228) — <code>&#42; Added option to specify in case of a 1 layer model (either quadtree input, or original input), whether you want the output as mesh quadtree netcdf (1), or regular gridded netcdf (0, default). | B096<br>netspwfile의 절대기압과 precipitation라는 대상 표기 | [docs/developments.rst:229](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:229) — <code>&#42; netspwfile input for precipitation should be absolute atmospheric pressure, not the pressure drop.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:968](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:968) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;inq&#95;varid(net&#95;file&#95;spw%ncid, "pressure",          net&#95;file&#95;spw%pressure&#95;varid) )   ! Note: absolute pressure, not pressure drop</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:972](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:972) — <code>status = NF90&#95;INQ&#95;VARID(net&#95;file&#95;spw%ncid, "precipitation", net&#95;file&#95;spw%precip&#95;varid)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:1044](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:1044) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;get&#95;var(net&#95;file&#95;spw%ncid, net&#95;file&#95;spw%pressure&#95;varid, prtmp, start = (/ 1, 1, it /), count = (/ spw&#95;ncols, spw&#95;nrows, 1 /))) ! be aware of start indices</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:1046](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:1046) — <code>spw&#95;pabs(it,:,:) = ampr&#95;prtmp(1,:,:)</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 절대기압 요구는 pressure 변수와 대응한다. 확인: precipitation은 별도 강수 변수이다. 해석: 문장의 precipitation은 pressure를 잘못 적은 대상 표기이다. 이 문장을 강수 단위 변경으로 해석할 수 없다. |
| B097<br>Green-Ampt 추가 | [docs/developments.rst:258](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:258) — <code>&#42; Green &amp; Ampt infiltration method</code> | [source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:838](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:838) — <code>do nm = 1, np</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:846](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:846) — <code>if (prcp(nm) &lt; ksfield(nm)) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:856](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:856) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = (ksfield(nm) &#42; (1.0 + (GA&#95;head(np) &#42; GA&#95;sigma(np)) / GA&#95;F(nm)))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:863](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:863) — <code>GA&#95;sigma(nm) = max(GA&#95;sigma(nm) - (qinfmap(nm) &#42; dt / GA&#95;Lu(nm)), 0.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:867](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:867) — <code>GA&#95;F(nm)    = GA&#95;F(nm) + qinfmap(nm) &#42; dt   ! internal cumulative rainfall from Green-Ampt</code> | **일치** | 확인: gai 분기에서 침투율과 토양 상태를 갱신한다. 제시한 기본식과 공간별 매개변수의 차이는 입력 설명 대조에 적는다. |
| B098<br>Horton 추가 | [docs/developments.rst:259](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:259) — <code>&#42; Horton infiltration method</code> | [source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:961](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:961) — <code>rain&#95;T1(nm) = rain&#95;T1(nm) - dt                                           ! negative amount of how long it is infiltrating</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:967](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:967) — <code>I = exp(horton&#95;kd(nm) &#42; rain&#95;T1(nm) / 3600)                                 ! note that horton&#95;kd is factor in hours while dt is seconds</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:979](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:979) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = (horton&#95;fc(nm) + (horton&#95;f0(nm) - horton&#95;fc(nm)) &#42; I) / 3600 / 1000 ! from mm/hr to m/s</code> | **일치** | 확인: hor 분기가 강우 지속 시간에 따른 침투능 감소를 계산한다. 확인: 시간은 초에서 시간 단위로 변환한다. |

Note: absolute pressure, not pressure drop</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:972](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncinput.F90:972) — <code>status = NF90&#95;INQ&#95;VARID(net&#95;file&#95;spw%ncid, "precipitation", net&#95;file&#95;spw%precip&#95;varid)</code> | **일치** | 확인: 현재 코드가 NetCDF spiderweb 입력을 읽는다. ASCII와 기압·바람 표현이 다르다. |
| B101<br>NetCDF source·discharge | [docs/developments.rst:262](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:262) — <code>&#42; Netcdf src+dis input option</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:264](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:264) — <code>call read&#95;char&#95;input(500,'netsrcdisfile',netsrcdisfile,'none')</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:59](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:59) — <code>call read&#95;netcdf&#95;discharge&#95;data()  ! reads nsrc, ntsrc, xsrc, ysrc, qsrc, and tsrc</code> | **일치** | 확인: NetCDF source·discharge 입력 선택과 리더 호출이 존재한다. 실제 FEWS 파일의 호환성은 파일 검증이 필요하다. |
| B102<br>암거·배수관의 계산식 | [docs/developments.rst:266](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:266) — <code>&#42; Improved culvert and drainage pipe formulations</code><br>[docs/input_structures.rst:207](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:207) — <code>&#96;&#96;par1 = &#92;(&#92;mu &#92;cdot A &#92;cdot &#92;sqrt{2g}&#92;)&#96;&#96;</code> | [source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:398](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:398) — <code>qq  = drainage&#95;params(idrn, 1) &#42; sqrt(zs(nmin) - zs(nmout))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:402](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:402) — <code>qq  = -drainage&#95;params(idrn, 1) &#42; sqrt(zs(nmout) - zs(nmin))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:422](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:422) — <code>qq = max(qq, 0.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:625](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:625) — <code>qq = 1.0 / (structure&#95;relax / dt) &#42; qq + (1.0 - (1.0 / (structure&#95;relax / dt))) &#42; -qtsrc(jin)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:632](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_discharges.f90:632) — <code>qq = min(qq, max(z&#95;volume(nmin), 0.0) / dt)</code> | **일치** | 확인: 코드는 par1·sqrt(&#124;수두차&#124;)와 방향 부호를 사용한다. 확인: 완화와 물량 제한을 적용한다. 과거 식에서 개선된 정도는 이 대조에서 확인하지 않았다. |
| B103<br>이류의 2차원 성분 개선 | [docs/developments.rst:283](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:283) — <code>&#42; Improved 2D component of advection scheme</code> | [source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:466](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:466) — <code>uu = (uu&#95;nm + uu&#95;nmu) / 2</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:478](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:478) — <code>dqyudy = qd &#42; (uu&#95;nm - uu&#95;ndm) &#42; dyuvinv</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:482](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:482) — <code>dqyudy = dqyudy + qu &#42; (uu&#95;num - uu&#95;nm) &#42; dyuvinv</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:491](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:491) — <code>dqyudy = dqyudy + ud &#42; ( qy&#95;nm - qy&#95;ndm ) &#42; dyuvinv</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:495](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:495) — <code>dqyudy = dqyudy + uu &#42; ( qy&#95;nmu - qy&#95;ndmu ) &#42; dyuvinv</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 현재 이류가 두 방향의 속도·유량 성분을 사용한다. 개선 전후 차이는 이전 소스가 있어야 판정할 수 있다. |
| B104<br>spiderweb 강수의 사용 선택 | [docs/developments.rst:284](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:284) — <code>&#42; Option to not use rainfall in spiderweb, keyword:  usespwprecip = 0</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:108](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:108) — <code>call read&#95;int&#95;input(500,'usespwprecip',ispwprecip,1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:601](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:601) — <code>use&#95;spw&#95;precip = .true.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:602](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:602) — <code>if (ispwprecip==0) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:603](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:603) — <code>use&#95;spw&#95;precip = .false.</code> | **일치** | 확인: usespwprecip=0이 spiderweb 강수 사용을 끈다. 확인: 현재 기본값은 1이다. |
| B105<br>weir 부착 좌표·높이 출력 | [docs/developments.rst:285](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:285) — <code>&#42; The x&amp;y-coordinates of input weirfiles as snapped on grid internally in SFINCS are now written to the sfincs&#95;his.nc file;  structure&#95;x, structure&#95;y &amp; structure&#95;height</code> | [source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:1900](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:1900) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;def&#95;var(his&#95;file%ncid, 'structure&#95;x', NF90&#95;FLOAT, (/his&#95;file%structures&#95;dimid/), his&#95;file%structure&#95;x&#95;varid))  ! snapped coordinate as used in sfincs</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:1906](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:1906) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;def&#95;var(his&#95;file%ncid, 'structure&#95;y', NF90&#95;FLOAT, (/his&#95;file%structures&#95;dimid/), his&#95;file%structure&#95;y&#95;varid))  ! snapped coordinate as used in sfincs</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:1912](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:1912) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;def&#95;var(his&#95;file%ncid, 'structure&#95;height', NF90&#95;FLOAT, (/his&#95;file%structures&#95;dimid/), his&#95;file%structure&#95;height&#95;varid)) ! structure height</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:2273](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:2273) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;put&#95;var(his&#95;file%ncid,  his&#95;file%structure&#95;x&#95;varid, struc&#95;x)) ! write structure&#95;x, input struc&#95;x</code> | **일치** | 확인: structure_x·structure_y·structure_height가 존재한다. 확인: 이 값은 격자에 부착한 구조물 위치와 높이이다. |
| B106<br>Blockhaus 점성 입력의 과거 의미 | [docs/developments.rst:286](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:286) — <code>&#42; Option to include viscosity, enabling running on theta=1.0,  with viscosity = 1. The values 'nuvisc' will be automatically determined based on your grid resolution, and written to the log screen. Value can still be overruled by specifying 'nuvisc = value' directly, or increased with e.g. a factor 2 using 'nuviscdim = 2'.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:110](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:110) — <code>call read&#95;real&#95;input(500,'nuvisc',nuviscdim,0.01)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:111) — <code>call read&#95;logical&#95;input(500,'viscosity',iviscosity,.false.)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1565](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1565) — <code>nuvisc(iref) = max(nuviscdim &#42; dxyr(iref), 0.0) ! take min of dx and dy, don't allow to be negative</code> | **문서에만** | 확인: 현재 코드가 nuviscdim을 입력 이름으로 읽지 않는다. 확인: 현재 nuvisc는 셀 길이에 곱하는 계수이다. 해석: 직접 계수·nuviscdim 배율 설명은 해당 과거 판의 이력으로 보존해야 한다. 현재 설정 안내로 적용하면 이름과 의미가 맞지 않는다. |
| B107<br>최대 속도 proxy | [docs/developments.rst:287](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:287) — <code>&#42; Save maximum velocity proxy (in m/s) on 'dtmaxout'  interval:   storevelmax = 1</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:277](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:277) — <code>call read&#95;int&#95;input(500,'storevelmax',storevelmax,0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:455](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:455) — <code>if (storevelmax==1 .and. dtmaxout&gt;0.0) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:456](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:456) — <code>store&#95;maximum&#95;velocity = .true.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:684](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:684) — <code>vmax(nm) = max(vmax(nm), uvz)</code> | **일치** | 확인: 최대 속도 저장 플래그와 dtmaxout 조건이 대응한다. 이 값은 셀 주변 유량에서 산정한 proxy이다. |
| B108<br>최대 단위폭 유량 | [docs/developments.rst:288](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:288) — <code>&#42; Save maximum flux  (h &#42; U in m^2/s) on 'dtmaxout'  interval:   storefluxmax = 1</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:278](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:278) — <code>call read&#95;int&#95;input(500,'storefluxmax',storefluxmax,0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:460](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:460) — <code>if (storefluxmax==1 .and. dtmaxout&gt;0.0) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:461](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:461) — <code>store&#95;maximum&#95;flux = .true.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:691](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:691) — <code>qmax(nm) = max(qmax(nm), qz)</code> | **일치** | 확인: 최대 유량 proxy의 저장 경로가 대응한다. 해석: 유량 배열 q의 단위는 m^2/s이다. |
| B109<br>배수 구조물의 최대 유량 출력 | [docs/developments.rst:289](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:289) — <code>&#42; Save maximum discharge through drainage strucuture from 'drnfile' input on 'dthisout'  interval:   storeqdrain = 1</code> | [source/src/sfincs_output.f90:660](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_output.f90:660) — <code>if (ndrn&gt;0 .and. store&#95;qdrain) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_output.f90:663](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_output.f90:663) — <code>write(970,'(f12.1,10000f9.3)')t,(qtsrc(iobs), iobs = nsrc + 1, nsrcdrn, 2)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3459](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3459) — <code>if (ndrn&gt;0) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3467](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3467) — <code>q&#95;drain(idrn) = qtsrc(iobs)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3470](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_ncoutput.F90:3470) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;put&#95;var(his&#95;file%ncid, his&#95;file%drain&#95;varid, q&#95;drain, (/1, nthisout/))) ! write discharge of sink point</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 이력 출력은 출력 시각의 qtsrc를 그대로 쓴다. 확인: 해당 경로에서 dthisout 구간의 최대값을 모으지 않는다. 문서의 'maximum discharge'가 현재 출력과 맞지 않는다. |
| B110<br>weir 계산식의 버그 수정 | [docs/developments.rst:290](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:290) — <code>&#42; Bugfix in weir formulation</code> | [source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:669](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:669) — <code>if (h2 &gt; 2.0 / 3.0 &#42; h1) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:673](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:673) — <code>qstruc = Cd&#42;h2&#42;sqrt(2.0 &#42; 9.81 &#42; (h1 - h2)/(1.0 - m))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:679](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:679) — <code>qstruc = Cd&#42;cweir&#42;h1&#42;&#42;1.5</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 현재 코드가 잠긴 월류와 자유 월류를 분기한다. 수정 전 식과 실패 사례가 없어 버그 수정 이력을 판정할 수 없다. |
| B111<br>subgrid와 OpenACC 추가 | [docs/developments.rst:301](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:301) — <code>The code consists of all functionality of v1, with the large addition of the subgrid mode and first GPU functionality using openacc.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535) — <code>z&#95;volume(nm) = z&#95;volume(nm) + dvol</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565) — <code>dzvol    = subgrid&#95;z&#95;volmax(nm) / (subgrid&#95;nlevels - 1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568) — <code>zs(nm)   = subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm) + (subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv + 1, nm) - subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm)) &#42; facint</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:625](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:625) — <code>!$acc parallel, present(zs, q, uv, structure&#95;uv&#95;index, uv&#95;index&#95;z&#95;nm, uv&#95;index&#95;z&#95;nmu, structure&#95;parameters, structure&#95;type, structure&#95;length)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:626](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:626) — <code>!$acc loop independent gang vector</code> | **일치** | 확인: 현재 소스에 subgrid 표 소비와 OpenACC 지시문이 존재한다. 최초 도입 판은 현재 tree만으로 독립 확인할 수 없다. |

### 4.4 subgrid 방법과 성능 서술

| 서술 | 문서(파일:줄, 원문 인용) | 코드(파일:줄, 원문 인용) | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B112<br>수위를 더 미세한 격자에서 갱신한다는 설명 | [docs/developments.rst:318](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:318) — <code>Subgrid features are a method in which flux computations are performed on a coarser grid than the update of the water levels which is done on a much finer resolution.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:337](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:337) — <code>do nm = 1, np</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535) — <code>z&#95;volume(nm) = z&#95;volume(nm) + dvol</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565) — <code>dzvol    = subgrid&#95;z&#95;volmax(nm) / (subgrid&#95;nlevels - 1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:566](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:566) — <code>iuv      = int(z&#95;volume(nm) / dzvol) + 1</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568) — <code>zs(nm)   = subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm) + (subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv + 1, nm) - subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm)) &#42; facint</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 연속식은 활성 계산 셀의 z_volume을 갱신한다. 확인: 셀의 subgrid 표에서 같은 계산 셀의 zs를 산정한다. 이 경로는 DEM 픽셀마다 별도 수위를 시간 적분하지 않는다. 문서는 높은 지형 해상도와 수위 계산 격자 해상도를 혼동한다. |
| B113<br>subgrid의 속도·정확도 이점 | [docs/developments.rst:319](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:319) — <code>In this way computations can be sped up, while still using high resolution information of topography and bathymetry.</code><br>[docs/developments.rst:330](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:330) — <code>This can be overcome by using a subgrid approach for the continuity update. This has the benefit that larger grid domains can be used while keeping accurate results.</code><br>[docs/developments.rst:338](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:338) — <code>The makes is possible to compute on a coarser grid resolution (improvement of efficiency) while still detailed information about the local elevation is incorporated when determining corresponding water levels leading to accurate results.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565) — <code>dzvol    = subgrid&#95;z&#95;volmax(nm) / (subgrid&#95;nlevels - 1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:566](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:566) — <code>iuv      = int(z&#95;volume(nm) / dzvol) + 1</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:567](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:567) — <code>facint   = (z&#95;volume(nm) - (iuv - 1) &#42; dzvol ) / dzvol</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568) — <code>zs(nm)   = subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm) + (subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv + 1, nm) - subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm)) &#42; facint</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:373](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:373) — <code>iuv    = min(int((zsu - zmin) / dzuv) + 1, subgrid&#95;nlevels - 1)                                         ! index of level below zsu</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:376](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:376) — <code>hu     = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;havg(iuv, ip) + (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;havg(iuv + 1, ip) - subgrid&#95;uv&#95;havg(iuv, ip)) &#42; facint   ! grid-average depth</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378) — <code>phi    = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip) + (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv + 1, ip) - subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip)) &#42; facint   ! wet fraction</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 현재 코드는 고해상도 지형에서 만든 표를 계산 셀에서 소비한다. 속도와 정확도 이점의 크기는 입력 표와 비교 결과가 있어야 판정할 수 있다. |
| B114<br>격자 2배 세분화의 2^3 시간 증가 | [docs/developments.rst:329](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:329) — <code>Often model runtimes are too large to go to very fine resolution modelling because refining a grid size with a factor 2, leads to a 2^3 longer model runtime due to the time step limitation in the CFL-criteria.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:111) — <code>dxr(iref) = quadtree&#95;dx / 2&#42;&#42;(iref - 1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:112) — <code>dyr(iref) = quadtree&#95;dy / 2&#42;&#42;(iref - 1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:731](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:731) — <code>min&#95;dt&#95;ip = 1.0 / ( max(sqrt(g &#42; hu), abs(uv(ip)) ) &#42; dxuvinv)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_lib.f90:391](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_lib.f90:391) — <code>dt = alfa &#42; min&#95;dt ! min&#95;dt was computed in sfincs&#95;momentum.f90 without alfa</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 셀 길이가 CFL 시간 간격에 들어간다. 해석: 2차원 셀 수 네 배와 시간 단계 두 배라는 가정에서 여덟 배 추정이 나온다. 실제 시간은 수심·속도·활성 셀·병렬 처리에도 의존한다. 실행 없이 여덟 배를 확정할 수 없다. |
| B115<br>전처리에서 지형 기반 subgrid 표를 작성 | [docs/developments.rst:334](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:334) — <code>The subgrid method implemented so that subgrid tables are derived in pre-processing that contain relations between the water level and volume for every grid cell.</code><br>[docs/developments.rst:335](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:335) — <code>These tables are derived using high resolution topography and bathymetry data.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:94](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:94) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;open(trim(sbgfile), NF90&#95;CLOBBER, net&#95;file&#95;sbg%ncid))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:108](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:108) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;inquire&#95;dimension(net&#95;file&#95;sbg%ncid, net&#95;file&#95;sbg%nlevels&#95;dimid, len = subgrid&#95;nlevels)) ! nr of levels</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:352](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_subgrid.F90:352) — <code>NF90(nf90&#95;get&#95;var(net&#95;file&#95;sbg%ncid, net&#95;file&#95;sbg%uv&#95;pwet&#95;varid, rtmpuv2(:,:) ))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568) — <code>zs(nm)   = subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm) + (subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv + 1, nm) - subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm)) &#42; facint</code> | **일치** | 확인: 현재 계산 kernel은 이미 작성된 표를 읽는다. 문서가 표 생성 단계를 전처리로 구분한다. 생성에 사용한 DEM 해상도와 표 생성 알고리즘은 범위 밖 전처리 자료가 필요하다. |
| B116<br>거친 격자 유량 뒤 subgrid 수위 산정 | [docs/developments.rst:336](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:336) — <code>In the SFINCS model itself, these subgrid tables are used to determine an accurate estimation of the water level after calculating fluxes on a coarser grid resolution.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535) — <code>z&#95;volume(nm) = z&#95;volume(nm) + dvol</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:565) — <code>dzvol    = subgrid&#95;z&#95;volmax(nm) / (subgrid&#95;nlevels - 1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568) — <code>zs(nm)   = subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm) + (subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv + 1, nm) - subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm)) &#42; facint</code> | **일치** | 확인: 유량 차이로 계산한 셀 부피를 수위 표에 대입한다. 정확도 수준은 별도 검증 결과가 필요하다. |
| B117<br>유량용 대표 수심 | [docs/developments.rst:337](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:337) — <code>Additionally, for calculating the fluxes between cells, a representative water depth is determined.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:351](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:351) — <code>hu = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;havg&#95;zmax(ip) + zsu</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:364](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:364) — <code>gnavg2 = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;navg&#95;w(ip) - (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;navg&#95;w(ip) - subgrid&#95;uv&#95;nrep&#95;zmax(ip)) / (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;fnfit(ip) &#42; (zsu - zmax) + 1.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:373](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:373) — <code>iuv    = min(int((zsu - zmin) / dzuv) + 1, subgrid&#95;nlevels - 1)                                         ! index of level below zsu</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:376](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:376) — <code>hu     = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;havg(iuv, ip) + (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;havg(iuv + 1, ip) - subgrid&#95;uv&#95;havg(iuv, ip)) &#42; facint   ! grid-average depth</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:378) — <code>phi    = subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip) + (subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv + 1, ip) - subgrid&#95;uv&#95;pwet(iuv, ip)) &#42; facint   ! wet fraction</code> | **일치** | 확인: 흐름 수심·대표 조도·젖은 비율을 표에서 보간한다. subgrid는 수위 계산용 표와 유량 계산용 표를 구분한다. |
| B118<br>200 m 대 100 m의 여덟 배 속도 향상 | [docs/developments.rst:342](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:342) — <code>Due to this time step limitation, if one can calculate fluxes on a 200 m grid instead of a 100m grid, the computational speedup is a factor 8.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:731](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:731) — <code>min&#95;dt&#95;ip = 1.0 / ( max(sqrt(g &#42; hu), abs(uv(ip)) ) &#42; dxuvinv)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_lib.f90:391](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_lib.f90:391) — <code>dt = alfa &#42; min&#95;dt ! min&#95;dt was computed in sfincs&#95;momentum.f90 without alfa</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: CFL 계산은 셀 길이를 사용한다. 코드는 실행 시간의 여덟 배 향상을 보장하지 않는다. 대상 지형·외력·컴파일 설정·계산 결과가 필요하다. |
| B119<br>Houston 사례에서 더 큰 속도 향상 | [docs/developments.rst:343](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:343) — <code>Our case study in Houston shows that even larger increases in speed are possible!</code><br>[docs/developments.rst:344](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:344) — <code>See: https://agu2020fallmeeting-agu.ipostersessions.com/Default.aspx?s=9C-05-18-CF-F1-2B-17-F0-7A-21-93-E6-13-AE-F3-24</code> | [source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:568) — <code>zs(nm)   = subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm) + (subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv + 1, nm) - subgrid&#95;z&#95;dep(iuv, nm)) &#42; facint</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 문서가 외부 사례를 링크한다. 링크의 결과와 실행 기록이 제공되지 않아 향상 배율을 판정할 수 없다. 범위 밖 자료를 열지 않았다. |
| B120<br>GPU 계산 지시문 | [docs/developments.rst:348](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/developments.rst:348) — <code>The SFINCS source code has now been GPU enabled to make optimal use of fast Graphics Processing Unit computers.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:142](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:142) — <code>!$acc parallel, present( kcuv, kfuv, zs, q, q0, uv, uv0, zsderv, &amp;</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:156](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_momentum.f90:156) — <code>!$acc loop, reduction( min : min&#95;dt ), gang, vector</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:625](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:625) — <code>!$acc parallel, present(zs, q, uv, structure&#95;uv&#95;index, uv&#95;index&#95;z&#95;nm, uv&#95;index&#95;z&#95;nmu, structure&#95;parameters, structure&#95;type, structure&#95;length)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:626](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:626) — <code>!$acc loop independent gang vector</code> | **일치** | 확인: 현재 source에 OpenACC 병렬 지시문이 존재한다. 지시문 존재는 배포 실행 파일의 GPU 활성화나 속도 향상을 증명하지 않는다. |

### 4.5 입력 문서의 계산식·저장·진단·구조물

| 서술 | 문서(파일:줄, 원문 인용) | 코드(파일:줄, 원문 인용) | 판정 | 이유 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| B121<br>Cartesian 정규격자만 사용한다는 제한 | [docs/input.rst:110](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:110) — <code>SFINCS uses a staggered equidistant recti-linear grid, grid sizes for x- a y-direction can be different. SFINCS can only be used in cartesian coordinates (e.g. UTM zone).</code><br>[docs/input.rst:111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:111) — <code>The grid is initialised by stating an origin location of the cell edges (x0, y0), a number of grid cells in x-&amp;y-direction (mmax, nmax) and the grid sizes in x-&amp;y-direction (dx,dy).</code><br>[docs/input.rst:112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:112) — <code>If desired the grid can also be rotated using 'rotation', in degrees from the x-axis (east) in anti-clockwise direction.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:104](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:104) — <code>call read&#95;int&#95;input(500,'crsgeo',igeo,0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:399](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:399) — <code>if (igeo == 0) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:419](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:419) — <code>crsgeo = .true.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:193](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:193) — <code>if (use&#95;quadtree) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:197](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:197) — <code>call quadtree&#95;read&#95;file(qtrfile, snapwave, nonhydrostatic)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1403](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1403) — <code>if (crsgeo) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1405](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_domain.f90:1405) — <code>! dxr and dyr are now in degrees. Must convert to metres.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:111](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:111) — <code>dxr(iref) = quadtree&#95;dx / 2&#42;&#42;(iref - 1)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:112](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:112) — <code>dyr(iref) = quadtree&#95;dy / 2&#42;&#42;(iref - 1)</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 코드는 quadtree 입력과 구면좌표 분기를 제공한다. 문서의 'only' 제한이 현재 코드와 맞지 않는다. 확인: 기존 투영 정규격자의 staggered 구조·원점·회전 설명은 대응한다. |
| B122<br>CN A의 유출 기본식 | [docs/input.rst:418](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:418) — <code>In its classic form, the Curve Number model uses the following equation to relate total event runoff Q to total event precipitation P as follows:</code><br>[docs/input.rst:420](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:420) — <code>&#42;&#42;S = (1000./CN - 10)&#42;&#42;</code><br>[docs/input.rst:422](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:422) — <code>&#42;&#42;Q = (P^2/(P+Smax))&#42;&#42;</code><br>[docs/input.rst:455](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:455) — <code>The user can also specify the sfacinf which controls the initial abstraction or the amount of water before runoff, such as infiltration, or rainfall interception by vegetation; historically, it has generally been assumed that sfacinf = 0.2 (default, however, for urbanized watersheds lower values can be expected (e.g. 0.05).</code> | [source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:290](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:290) — <code>qinffield = qinffield &#42; 0.0254   ! to m</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:689](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:689) — <code>if (cumprcp(nm) &gt; sfacinf &#42; qinffield(nm)) then ! qinffield is S</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:693](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:693) — <code>Qq  = (cumprcp(nm) - sfacinf &#42; qinffield(nm))&#42;&#42;2 / (cumprcp(nm) + (1.0 - sfacinf) &#42; qinffield(nm))  ! cumulative runoff in m</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:701](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:701) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = prcp(nm)</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 문서는 Q=P^2/(P+Smax)를 적는다. 확인: 현재 코드는 Ia=sfacinf·S를 뺀 누적 유출 식을 사용한다. 확인: 기본 sfacinf는 0.2이다. 해석: 문서의 간단한 식은 초기 손실이 0인 경우로 한정해야 한다. 확인: CN→S의 변환은 전처리 지시이며 kernel은 S를 읽는다. |
| B123<br>Green-Ampt 식과 셀별 매개변수 | [docs/input.rst:492](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:492) — <code>The Green-Ampt method is a hydrological model extensively used to predict soil infiltration rates under ponded conditions, i.e., when water is freely available on the surface. This method considers important parameters such as initial soil moisture content, hydraulic conductivity, and soil capillary suction head.</code><br>[docs/input.rst:496](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:496) — <code>&#42;&#42;f(t) = K(1+ delta&#95;theta (sigma + h0) / F(t) )&#42;&#42;</code><br>[docs/input.rst:499](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:499) — <code>In which t is time, K is the saturated hydraulic conductivity, delta&#95;theta is defined as the soil capacity (the difference between the saturated and initial moisture content) and sigma is the soil suction head.</code><br>[docs/input.rst:501](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:501) — <code>Within SFINCS, the Green-Ampt method can be used as follows. The user needs to provide the following variables. For a range of typical values see Table 1. For all variables, one needs to specify these values per cell with the same grid based input as the depfile using a binary file:</code><br>[docs/input.rst:504](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:504) — <code>&#42; sigmafile: soil moisture deficit in &#91;-&#93;</code><br>[docs/input.rst:505](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:505) — <code>&#42; psifile: suction head at the wetting front in mm</code> | [source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:838](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:838) — <code>do nm = 1, np</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:846](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:846) — <code>if (prcp(nm) &lt; ksfield(nm)) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:850](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:850) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = prcp(nm)                       ! infiltration is same as rainfall</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:856](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:856) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = (ksfield(nm) &#42; (1.0 + (GA&#95;head(np) &#42; GA&#95;sigma(np)) / GA&#95;F(nm)))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:857](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:857) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = max(min(qinfmap(nm), prcp(nm)), 0.0)     ! never more than rainfall and and never negative</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:863](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:863) — <code>GA&#95;sigma(nm) = max(GA&#95;sigma(nm) - (qinfmap(nm) &#42; dt / GA&#95;Lu(nm)), 0.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:867](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:867) — <code>GA&#95;F(nm)    = GA&#95;F(nm) + qinfmap(nm) &#42; dt   ! internal cumulative rainfall from Green-Ampt</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 제시식은 흡입수두에 표면 수심 h0를 더한다. 확인: 코드의 고강수 분기는 GA_head·GA_sigma/F만 사용한다. 확인: 같은 분기가 GA_head(np)·GA_sigma(np)를 참조한다. 확인: ksfield와 GA_F는 nm를 참조한다. 해석: 문서의 셀별 매개변수 의미와 마지막 셀 참조가 다르다. 해석: 전체 침투율이 공간적으로 균일해진다는 뜻은 아니다. |
| B124<br>저장용량을 모든 유입보다 먼저 사용한다는 설명 | [docs/input.rst:539](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:539) — <code>Storage volume for green infrastructure on the 2D grid, specifies the maximum storage volume of water per grid cell in cubic meters.</code><br>[docs/input.rst:540](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:540) — <code>Water will be stored in this storage volume before it will contribute to surface runoff.</code><br>[docs/input.rst:542](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:542) — <code>&#42;&#42;NOTE - Only useable in subgrid mode (on either regular or quadtree grid)&#42;&#42;</code> | [source/src/sfincs_input.f90:655](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:655) — <code>if (volfile(1:4) /= 'none') then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:656](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:656) — <code>if (subgrid) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_input.f90:657](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_input.f90:657) — <code>use&#95;storage&#95;volume = .true.</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:319](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:319) — <code>z&#95;volume(nm) = z&#95;volume(nm) + qtsrc(isrc) &#42; dt</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:501](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:501) — <code>! If water enters the cell through a point discharge, it will NOT end up in storage volume !</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:503](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:503) — <code>if (storage&#95;volume(nm) &gt; 1.0e-6 .and. dvol &gt; 0.0) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:513](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:513) — <code>storage&#95;volume(nm) = max(dv, 0.0)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:525](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:525) — <code>dvol = 0.0</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_continuity.f90:535) — <code>z&#95;volume(nm) = z&#95;volume(nm) + dvol</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 순강수·면 유량으로 계산한 양의 유입은 저장용량을 먼저 채운다. 확인: 점 유량은 z_volume에 직접 더한다. 확인: 코드 주석도 점 유량의 저장용량 우회를 적는다. 문서의 일반적인 우선 저장 설명이 이 예외를 누락한다. |
| B125<br>average_required_timestep의 평균·건조 값 | [docs/input.rst:757](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:757) — <code>average&#95;required&#95;timestep</code><br>[docs/input.rst:758](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:758) — <code>:description:&#9;&#9;The time-averaged CFL-limited timestep at each cell (scaled by the Courant factor &#96;&#96;alpha&#96;&#96;), taking the minimum across surrounding U/V flux points. Cells that were never wet during the simulation are assigned a value of -1.</code><br>[docs/input.rst:759](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:759) — <code>:units:&#9;&#9;s</code> | [source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:17](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:17) — <code>timestep&#95;analysis&#95;average&#95;required&#95;timestep = 0.0</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:51](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:51) — <code>timestep&#95;analysis&#95;average&#95;required&#95;timestep(ip) = timestep&#95;analysis&#95;average&#95;required&#95;timestep(ip) + timestep&#95;analysis&#95;required&#95;timestep(ip)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:55](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:55) — <code>timestep&#95;analysis&#95;times&#95;wet(ip) = timestep&#95;analysis&#95;times&#95;wet(ip) + 1</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:124](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:124) — <code>dtm = min(dtm, timestep&#95;analysis&#95;average&#95;required&#95;timestep(nmd1) / max(timestep&#95;analysis&#95;times&#95;wet(nmd1), 1))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:182](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:182) — <code>timestep&#95;analysis&#95;average&#95;required&#95;timestep&#95;per&#95;cell(nm) = min(dtm &#42; alfa, dtmax)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:188](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:188) — <code>timestep&#95;analysis&#95;average&#95;required&#95;timestep&#95;per&#95;cell(nm) = -1.0</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 코드는 젖은 U/V 면의 요구 시간 간격을 단계별로 합한다. 확인: 코드는 젖은 단계 수로 나눈 뒤 주변 면의 최솟값을 취한다. 확인: 유효 주변 면의 젖은 단계 수가 0이면 0/max(0,1)=0이 계산될 수 있다. 확인: -1은 dtm이 큰 초기값으로 남을 때만 기록한다. 문서의 'never wet이면 -1'이라는 일반 조건이 코드와 맞지 않는다. 해석: 이 평균은 dt 가중 시간 평균이 아니라 젖은 단계의 산술평균이다. |
| B126<br>percentage_limiting_timestep의 셀 빈도 | [docs/input.rst:761](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:761) — <code>percentage&#95;limiting&#95;timestep</code><br>[docs/input.rst:762](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:762) — <code>:description:&#9;&#9;The percentage of total simulation timesteps during which a cell's required timestep was the global minimum (i.e., it was the bottleneck). High values indicate cells that frequently limit the overall model performance.</code><br>[docs/input.rst:763](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:763) — <code>:units:&#9;&#9;%</code> | [source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:59](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:59) — <code>if (timestep&#95;analysis&#95;required&#95;timestep(ip) &lt;= min&#95;dt + 1.0e-6) then</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:61](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:61) — <code>timestep&#95;analysis&#95;times&#95;limiting(ip) = timestep&#95;analysis&#95;times&#95;limiting(ip) + 1</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:125](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:125) — <code>tmsl = max(tmsl, timestep&#95;analysis&#95;times&#95;limiting(nmd1))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:139](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:139) — <code>tmsl = max(tmsl, timestep&#95;analysis&#95;times&#95;limiting(nmu1))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:192](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_timestep_analysis.f90:192) — <code>timestep&#95;analysis&#95;percentage&#95;limiting&#95;per&#95;cell(nm) = 100.0 &#42; tmsl / nt</code> | **불일치** | 확인: 코드가 주변 면별 제한 횟수 중 최댓값을 전체 단계 수 nt로 나눈다. 문서는 셀이 제한한 전체 단계의 비율로 설명한다. 해석: 서로 다른 면이 서로 다른 단계에 제한하면 횟수의 최댓값과 단계 집합의 합집합 횟수가 달라질 수 있다. 실행 결과로 실제 차이를 측정하지 않았다. |
| B127<br>Horton 침투능 감소와 회복 | [docs/input.rst:517](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:517) — <code>According to the Horton Method, initially, the infiltration capacity is high, but it decreases exponentially over time until it reaches a stable, steady-state value, known as the final infiltration rate. This phenomenon occurs due to the gradual saturation of the soil, and the reduction in infiltration rate continues until it equals the soil's hydraulic conductivity.</code><br>[docs/input.rst:521](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:521) — <code>&#42;&#42;f&#95;t = f&#95;c + (f&#95;0 - f&#95;c) e^{-kt}&#42;&#42;</code><br>[docs/input.rst:527](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:527) — <code>&#42; f0file: maximum (Initial) Infiltration Capacity in mm/hr</code><br>[docs/input.rst:528](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:528) — <code>&#42; fcfile: Minimum (Asymptotic) Infiltration Rate in mm/hr</code><br>[docs/input.rst:529](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:529) — <code>&#42; kdfile: empirical constant (hr-1) of decay</code><br>[docs/input.rst:531](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input.rst:531) — <code>The recovery of the infiltration rate during dry weather (kr) is calculated as factor of the empirical decay constant. By default, this keyword (horton&#95;kr&#95;kd) is set to 10.0 meaning that the recovery goes 10 times as slow as the decay.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:961](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:961) — <code>rain&#95;T1(nm) = rain&#95;T1(nm) - dt                                           ! negative amount of how long it is infiltrating</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:967](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:967) — <code>I = exp(horton&#95;kd(nm) &#42; rain&#95;T1(nm) / 3600)                                 ! note that horton&#95;kd is factor in hours while dt is seconds</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:975](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:975) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = horton&#95;fc(nm) / 3600 / 1000   ! from mm/hr to m/s</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:979](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:979) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = (horton&#95;fc(nm) + (horton&#95;f0(nm) - horton&#95;fc(nm)) &#42; I) / 3600 / 1000 ! from mm/hr to m/s</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:1002](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:1002) — <code>qinfmap(nm) = qinfmap(nm) &#42; Qq / I                             ! scale Horton if capacity &gt; available</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:1010](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_infiltration.f90:1010) — <code>rain&#95;T1(nm) = rain&#95;T1(nm) + dt / horton&#95;kr&#95;kd                 ! positive amount of how long it is infiltrating</code> | **일치** | 확인: 강우 시간 rain_T1을 음수로 누적해서 지수 침투능을 감소시킨다. 확인: 이용 가능한 물량으로 침투량을 제한한다. 확인: 건조 시 dt/horton_kr_kd로 상태 시간을 회복한다. 기본 10은 감소보다 느린 회복을 뜻한다. |

| B130<br>thin dam의 입력점 상한 5000 | [docs/input_structures.rst:30](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:30) — <code>One can provide multiple polylines within one file, a maximum of 5000 supplied points is supported.</code> | [source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:445](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:445) — <code>read(500,&#42;,iostat = stat)nrows,ncols</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:447](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:447) — <code>allocate(xthd(nrows))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:448](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:448) — <code>allocate(ythd(nrows))</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:450](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:450) — <code>read(500,&#42;)xthd(irow),ythd(irow)</code><br>[source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:453](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:453) — <code>call find&#95;uv&#95;points&#95;intersected&#95;by&#95;polyline(uv&#95;indices, vertices, nr&#95;points, xthd, ythd, nrows)</code> | **확인불가** | 확인: 현재 호출부는 입력 nrows에 따라 좌표 배열을 할당한다. 이 줄에는 5000 입력점 검사가 없다. 교차점 보조 루틴의 제약까지 확인하려면 해당 의존성 원문이 필요하다. 지정된 source/src 범위를 확대하지 않았다.<br>[10-12 결론 정정] 교차점 보조 루틴의 원문이 지정된 source/src 안에 있다. `source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:853` — `subroutine find_uv_points_intersected_by_polyline(uv_indices,vertices,nint,xpol,ypol,npol)`; `source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:863` — `real*4, dimension(npol),              intent(in)  :: xpol,ypol`; `source/src/sfincs_quadtree.F90:884` — `do ipol = 1, npol - 1`. 확인: 호출부는 `source/src/sfincs_structures.f90:447` — `allocate(xthd(nrows))`로 입력점 수만큼 좌표를 할당한다. 해석: 이 읽기·교차점 경로는 고정 5000점 상한을 적용하지 않으므로 B로 결론낸다. |
| B131<br>weir의 높이·계수·잠김 분기 | [docs/input_structures.rst:80](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:80) — <code>Weirs are in principle the same as a thin dam, but then with a certain height (levee).</code><br>[docs/input_structures.rst:81](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:81) — <code>When the water level on either or both sides of the weir are higher than that of the weir, a flux over the weir is calculated.</code><br>[docs/input_structures.rst:82](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:82) — <code>Hereby a situation where the weir is partly or fully submerged is distinguished.</code><br>[docs/input_structures.rst:83](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:83) — <code>Besides the x&amp;y locations per points, also the elevation z and a Cd coefficient for the weir formula (recommended to use 0.6).</code><br>[docs/input_structures.rst:84](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:84) — <code>The supplied polylines are snapped onto the SFINCS grid within the model.</code><br>[docs/input_structures.rst:89](/home/firesinger/coastal-wiki/models/SFINCS/raw/source_code/sfincs/docs/input_structures.rst:89) — <code>The snapped coordinates are available in sfincs&#95;his.nc as structure&#95;x, structure&#95;y &amp;
